- `model` field = agent target (`openclaw` या `openclaw/<agentId>`); headers `x-openclaw-model` / `x-openclaw-agent-id` / `x-openclaw-session-key`

#### OpenMausBot से harvest (Apache-2.0 → NOTICE रखना)
| File | क्या मिलता है |
|---|---|
| `src/components/DesktopViewer.tsx` + `src/lib/desktop-connection.ts` | **noVNC 1.7.0 RFB live view** — keyboard/clipboard/fullscreen। ← यही तुम्हारा *"computer चलते हुए देख पाऊँ, mouse move हो"* है |
| `src/components/LocalScreenPreview.tsx` + `electron/screen-preview.cjs` + `server/computer-frames.ts` | Electron `desktopCapturer`/`getDisplayMedia` से host screen preview (बिना VM) |
| `src/components/ComputerPanel.tsx`, `ComputerFilesPane.tsx` | computer session UI |
| `scripts/prepare-cua-win.mjs`, `electron/cua.mjs`, `electron-builder.yml` का `win.extraResources` pattern | Windows CUA driver staging — **सिर्फ़ अगर Phase 0(A) पास हो** |

#### OpenDots से harvest (MIT → copyright रखना)
| File | क्या मिलता है |
|---|---|
| `SpaceLibrary.tsx`, `SpaceWorkspace.tsx`, `SpaceNav.tsx` + `src/client/editor/` (TipTap) | **"Spaces"** — Notion जैसा nested page library, तुम्हारे working documents के लिए |
| `src/server/store.ts` का `tasks`/`runs` scheduler shape (`intervalSeconds`, `nextRunAt`, lease-based, `"Interrupted"` state) + `TaskActions.tsx`, `TaskPresentation.tsx` | **scheduled/recurring responsibilities** — dots की पहचान |

#### OpenDots से जो **नहीं** लेना
`@copilotkit/*`, `CPK_INTELLIGENCE_API_KEY` (cloud mandatory), `src/server/dot-agent.ts`, `composio-tools.ts`, `slack-channel.ts`, `voice.ts`, `setup-telemetry.ts` + `docs/telemetry/*.hogql.sql` (PostHog ClickHouse queries — pure vendor cruft), `PageReviewCard.tsx` / `ConnectionActionCard.tsx` (approval cards — तुम्हें नहीं चाहिए)

> **एक exception:** `ask_user` जैसा सीधा सवाल तुम तक पहुंचना चाहिए। OMB भी यही करता है — full access mode में भी यह gate रहता है।

#### खुद बनाना है
Dot picker · live tool-call stream · session/thread list · skills browser+editor · settings/model picker

> 💡 **सस्ता विकल्प:** OpenClaw का अपना `ui/` (`openclaw-control-ui`, SolidJS 2 + Vite) Gateway से serve होता है और उसमें **पहले से noVNC desktop panel + ghostty-web terminal + CodeMirror skills editor + Plugins panel** है। अगर Phase 5 भारी लगे तो शुरुआत यहां से करो और बाद में अपना shell बनाओ। पर React नहीं है (SolidJS) और "Dots" जैसा UX खुद बनाना पड़ेगा।

---

### Phase 6 — Always-on + Proactive (असली "dots" behaviour) · 2–3 दिन

ChatGPT dots को distinctive क्या बनाता है: always-on, message **या call** करो, हर dot का अपना computer + persistent memory + ongoing responsibilities जिन पर वह **proactively** act करता है।

- **Always-on:** Gateway daemon = Windows Scheduled Task (boot + logon triggers) ✓ Phase 1 में हो गया
- **Scheduled/proactive:** Phase 5 का task scheduler + cron triggers। Reference: hermes के `/heartbeat`, `cron/`, `/goal`, `/loop`
- **Voice / call:** OpenClaw में `voice-call` channel + `talk.*` node commands पहले से हैं → "अपने dot को call करो" feature
- **Channels:** 20+ में से सिर्फ़ जो use करोगे वही रखो (अपना frontend + शायद Telegram/WhatsApp mobile के लिए)। बाकी ~150 extensions delete
- **Mobile (optional):** `apps/ios` (14.2 MiB) रख सकते हो अगर cross-surface चाहिए — dots का एक हिस्सा यही है

---

### Phase 7 — Cruft Strip + Harden · 1–2 दिन

**Delete करो (वज़न कम करने के लिए, नाम बदलने के लिए नहीं):**

| Target | Size |
|---|---|
| `ui/src/i18n/.i18n/*.tm.jsonl` (21 files) | **~100 MiB — repo के सबसे बड़े blobs** |
| `apps/` (ios 14.2, macos 13.8, android 11.7, linux, swabble, shared) | 58 MiB |
| `test/` (sqlite fixtures सहित) | 31 MiB |
| `docs/` | 31 MiB |
| `scripts/` | 21.7 MiB |
| `CHANGELOG/` (159 files) | 8.8 MiB |
| `extensions/qa-lab` | 6.6 MiB |
| `.github/`, `.agents/`, `qa/`, `crates/` (Rust) | ~14 MiB |
| ~150 unused channel/provider extensions | — |
| Cloud: `extensions/crabbox`, `deploy/`, `fly.toml`, `render.yaml`, `cloud-workers*`, `cloud-sessions` | — |
| `apps/{ios,macos}/.../Localizable.xcstrings` | 3.6 MB each |

**Principle: code delete करने के बजाय config से disable करो** — इससे upstream merges साफ़ रहते हैं।

**Telemetry:** daily `GET https://telemetry.openclaw.ai/api/latest-version` (UA `openclaw/<ver> (<os>; node/<v>; <arch>; gateway)`, कोई body/machine-ID नहीं)। Anonymous feature stats **opt-in** हैं। दोनों बंद: `update.checkOnStart: false`। Inspect: `openclaw telemetry show`।

**Security — निजी use में भी यह करो:**
1. `gateway.auth.mode: token` रखो (Phase 1 वाला नोट)
2. Gateway **हमेशा `127.0.0.1` पर** — port 18789 कभी expose न करो
3. ⚠️ **असली खतरा:** full-access + web browsing = **prompt injection → arbitrary shell**। एक malicious webpage agent को command दे सकती है। VM पर egress allowlist लगाओ, और `operator` dot को web + host-exec दोनों एक साथ मत दो

---

### Phase 8 — Legal / Attribution · 0.5 दिन

| Source | Obligation |
|---|---|
| OpenClaw (MIT) | `LICENSE` + `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` रखो |
| hermes-agent (MIT) | copyright notice रखो — **TypeScript port में भी** |
| OpenDots (MIT) | `Copyright (c) Atai Barkai` + `deployment/computers/LICENSE.openbot` रखो |
| **OpenMausBot (Apache-2.0)** | `LICENSE` + **`NOTICE` verbatim** रखो (इसमें MIT→Apache relicensing दर्ज है) और changes state करो। **Apache code Apache ही रहता है → तुम्हारा combined project Apache-2.0 होगा।** `enterprise/` **Apache नहीं है** (proprietary) — delete करो, `LICENSING.md` कहता है इससे OSS edition बन जाता है |
| noVNC (MPL-2.0) | file-level copyleft — अलग files में रखो, ठीक हो |
| cua-driver | MIT + Inter OFL + 7 MPL-2.0 crates (`third_party/cua-driver/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`, `SBOM.cdx.json`) |

**नाम:** "Dots" contested है — OpenAI ने 2026-09-29 को announce किया, उससे पहले एक US "DOTS" application मौजूद है (April 2025, AI chatbot class), और Amazon का "Dot" overlap करता है। "OpenMausBot"/"MausBot" + mascot **Supamaus Software Private Limited के trademarks हैं** और Apache §6 trademark rights नहीं देता। "Grok" xAI का है। → **अपना नाम चुनो** (surface branding से यही होगा)।

## 6. तुम्हारे सवालों के सीधे जवाब

**"Open Gateway से multiple providers — फायदेमंद होगा?"**
→ **हाँ, बहुत।** तीन जगह फायदा:
1. `models.providers.opengw` में एक entry से सारे providers, `models.mode: "merge"` से बाकी bundled providers भी साथ चलेंगे
2. **Failover:** auth-profile rotation → `agents.defaults.model.fallbacks` (`src/agents/failover/`) — एक provider गिरे तो दूसरा उठेगा
3. **सबसे बड़ा फायदा — hermes का auxiliary-slots pattern port करो:** compression / vision / curator / session_search / background-review के लिए **अलग-अलग सस्ता model**। भारी model सिर्फ़ main reasoning पर। Multi-provider setup का असली लाभ यही है, और OpenClaw में per-agent model (`agents.entries.*.model`) + `agents.defaults.modelPolicy.allow` पहले से है।

**"Skills में external add कर पाऊँगा, correct कर पाऊँगा, steps edit कर पाऊँगा?"**
→ **हाँ, सब पहले से built-in है** (Phase 4a)। CLI install (git/path/ZIP/registry), file-watcher से live reload, Control UI में revisions/undo वाला editor, और agent खुद `skill_workshop` tool से skills लिख सकता है। **कुछ बनाना नहीं पड़ेगा।** Plugins भी (`packages/plugin-sdk`, `register(api)` → tools/channels/providers/hooks/HTTP routes/Gateway methods/CLI/services/migrations register कर सकते हो)।

**"OpenDots को base बना लें?"**
→ **नहीं।** चार ठोस वजह: (1) `CPK_INTELLIGENCE_API_KEY` के बिना `requireReady()` throw करता है — कोई conversation नहीं चलती, और "local Intelligence" के लिए भी CopilotKit sign-in + internet चाहिए; (2) live computer stream नहीं — `ComputerPanel.tsx` सिर्फ़ manually-refreshed base64 PNG दिखाता है; (3) कोई host desktop control नहीं (container में headless browser); (4) approval cards design का core हैं, तुम्हें नहीं चाहिए। → **सिर्फ़ UX blueprint + Spaces/scheduler harvest।**

**"OpenMausBot को fork कर लें?"**
→ **पूरा नहीं।** उसका control layer OpenClaw के `extensions/cua-computer` से duplicate है (दोनों cua-driver)। OMB के 290 components bots/teams/cloud/enterprise concepts से भरे हैं — हल्की trimming नहीं, भारी deletion लगेगा। और OpenClaw से जोड़ने के लिए नया `ProviderDriver` या ACP bridge लिखना पड़ेगा। → **सिर्फ 4 चीज़ें harvest** (Phase 5)।

---

## 7. Top Risks

| # | Risk | Level | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Windows host control कहीं proven नहीं (OMB के docs खुद कहते हैं, OpenClaw का extension experimental है) | **HIGH** | Phase 0 GO/NO-GO gate + VM fallback (जो proven है) |
| 2 | OpenClaw upstream churn — सारे plugin APIs "experimental", 8,998 open issues, daily pushes | **HIGH** | Surface-only branding (तुम्हारा चुनाव) + config-disabling + upstream remote रखना + अपने code को plugins/workspace में अलग रखना |
| 3 | Prompt injection → arbitrary shell (full-access + browsing) | **HIGH** | VM egress allowlist; `operator` dot को web + host-exec एक साथ मत दो; gateway token |
| 4 | OpenClaw का आकार — 60,793 files, pnpm-only, aggressive CI (CodeQL boundary tests, semgrep, knip, oxlint/oxfmt, pre-commit) | MED | `--depth 1`, CI को अपने fork में हल्का करो, सिर्फ़ जिस हिस्से को छुओ वही समझो |
| 5 | CopilotKit Intelligence mandatory | — | Resolve हो गया — OpenDots को base नहीं बना रहे |
| 6 | Node 24.18.0 नहीं चलेगा | LOW | Phase 1 से पहले upgrade |

---

## 8. अगला कदम

**Phase 0 चलाओ** — तीन commands, एक दिन, और पता चल जाएगा कि पूरा plan किस route पर बनेगा। उसके बाद Phase 1। 
Plan तैयार है — ऊपर पूरा summary और file में detailed version।

सबसे ज़रूरी 3 बातें:
1. Merge मत करो, kernel + plugins करो — OpenClaw baseline, बाकी तीनों से design/concepts। यही 12 हफ़्ते vs 6 महीने का फ़र्क़ है।
2. असली काम तीन gaps में है — Windows computer-use (UIA + vision, सिर्फ़ mouse नहीं), team orchestrator, और अपनी UI (CopilotKit Intelligence हटाकर)।
3. V0 पहले — एक weekend में vanilla OpenClaw + OpenRouter + computer-use MCP चला कर देख लो। अगर वही 80% काम कर दे, तो fork का scope छोटा हो जाएगा।
अगला step बताइए: V0 run या Phase 0 scaffold?  मैंने तुम्हारे काउंटर प्लान के, तुमने जो प्लान बनाया, उसके काउंटर में तीन और नए प्लान बनाके तुम्हें दे रहा हूँ। काउंटर क्वेश्चन कर रहा हूँ मैं तुमको तीन नए प्लान के साथ। नया प्लान मतलब और देखो, अब तुम एक नया प्लान बनाव। देखो मैंने तुम्हें काउंटर प्लान दिया है। क्या ये सही है, क्या गलत है, ये बताओ। और बनाओ एक और, क्या कर सकते हो तुम इसके अलावा.

planner

आपका counter-plan पहले वाले से ज्यादा ठोस है। इसकी सबसे अच्छी बात है **“पहले V0, फिर तय करें कितना fork करना है”**। इससे पहले ही हफ्तों का UI या orchestration code लिखने से बचेंगे। लेकिन plan में कुछ दावे पक्के तथ्य की तरह लिखे हैं जबकि वे या तो प्रयोगात्मक हैं या repo में जाँचने होंगे।

## क्या सही है

- **Literal merge न करना:** OpenClaw को backend baseline रखकर बाकी repos से चुने हुए विचार या components लेना उचित है। अलग-अलग systems के agent loop, state और integrations को एक साथ जोड़ना maintenance बढ़ाएगा।
- **तीन असली काम पहचानना:** UI, multi-agent coordination और Windows computer control—इनमें सबसे ज्यादा अनिश्चितता है। OpenClaw में Windows/Linux का `cua-computer` मौजूद है, पर docs इसे **experimental** कहते हैं और इसे interactive desktop session में चलाना पड़ता है। इसलिए इसे “हल हुआ” नहीं मानना चाहिए। [OpenClaw computer-use docs](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/docs/nodes/computer-use.md)
- **OpenDots को बिना जाँच backend न बनाना:** उसका UI और product structure उपयोगी reference है। मगर conversations, computer service और deployment की निर्भरताएँ समझे बिना सीधे fork करने से CopilotKit services पर निर्भरता आ सकती है। OpenDots README hosted Intelligence, local evaluation और licensed self-hosting में फर्क बताता है। [OpenDots README](https://github.com/CopilotKit/OpenDots)
- **Multi-provider gateway उपयोगी है:** OpenClaw में provider configuration और per-agent model चुनने की सुविधा है। Gateway से अलग agents या कामों को अलग models पर चलाना और जरूरत पर fallback रखना फायदेमंद हो सकता है। पहले gateway का वास्तविक नाम और API compatibility पक्की करें—अगर आपका मतलब OpenRouter है, तो उसे OpenClaw में configure करके जाँचें। [OpenClaw provider docs](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/docs/providers/index.md)
- **External skills की गुंजाइश है:** OpenClaw skills को स्थानीय, Git और registry sources से install कर सकता है; Skill Workshop proposals बनाने और review करने का रास्ता देता है। लेकिन skill को अपने-आप trusted या सुरक्षित नहीं मानना चाहिए। [OpenClaw skills docs](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/docs/tools/skills.md)

## क्या सुधारना चाहिए

1. **“OpenClaw में सब built-in है” कहना ज्यादा आशावादी है।** Subagents और skills जैसी सुविधाएँ मिलती हैं, लेकिन आपके बताए persistent task board, mailbox, per-task budgets, सुंदर approvals UI और agent team coordination को तैयार product feature मानकर नहीं चलना चाहिए। उन्हें V0 में परखें और जो कमी निकले, उसी के लिए छोटा extension बनाएं।

2. **“सिर्फ़ mouse वाला 60–70% और UIA+verify से 90%+” जैसे success प्रतिशत अभी प्रमाणित नहीं हैं।** ये आपके Windows, ऐप्स, display scaling और चुने models पर निर्भर होंगे। अपने कामों की छोटी evaluation सूची बनाकर ही सफलता मापें।

3. **V0 का लक्ष्य थोड़ा बदलें।** Telegram तभी जोड़ें जब Telegram आपका वास्तविक interface हो। पहले OpenClaw का local chat/control UI और computer-use MCP को अलग test करें। Computer-use को अपने मुख्य Windows desktop पर full access देकर शुरुआत न करें—पहले isolated VM या test account में “Notepad खोलो, टेक्स्ट लिखो, सेव करो, फिर पढ़कर verify करो” चलाएँ।

4. **Permission UI हटाना और safeguards हटाना अलग बातें हैं।** आप अकेले user हैं, इसलिए multi-user approval/pairing UX की जरूरत शायद न हो। फिर भी kill switch, API budget cap, audit trail, local gateway authentication और destructive कामों का रोक/confirmation रखना चाहिए। Dedicated system गलती या malicious webpage से सुरक्षित नहीं हो जाता; OpenClaw की docs भी screen content को untrusted बताती हैं। [OpenClaw computer-use security notes](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/docs/nodes/computer-use.md)

5. **Skills की “सीधी compatibility” को सीमित दावे की तरह लें।** समान `SKILL.md` format होने से skill निर्देश पढ़े जा सकते हैं; इससे यह गारंटी नहीं मिलती कि उसमें माँगे गए binaries, APIs, tools या दूसरे agent-specific व्यवहार उपलब्ध होंगे। Hermes से सैकड़ों skills bulk-import करना भी पहला कदम नहीं होना चाहिए। 3–5 उपयोगी skills चुनें, source/license/dependencies जाँचें और फिर import करें।

6. **समय का अनुमान कम है।** 16–25 दिन में installable, सुरक्षित, Windows desktop control वाला, multi-agent और polished निजी assistant बन जाना बहुत आशावादी है—खासकर अगर आप अकेले और part-time काम कर रहे हैं। एक weekend का V0 संभव है। उसके बाद minimal निजी prototype में कुछ हफ्ते लग सकते हैं; polished app और भरोसेमंद desktop automation को कई iteration चाहिए। 12–14 सप्ताह भी तभी ठीक अनुमान है जब scope सीमित हो और नियमित development समय मिले।

7. **कुछ implementation विवरण अभी निर्णय नहीं हैं।** Node, pnpm, Python/C#, port numbers, database schema और Electron shell को अभी fixed requirements न बनाएं। पहले वर्तमान OpenClaw release के install/build निर्देश और computer-use path जाँचें। Python बनाम C# service और React बनाम मौजूदा Control UI का निर्णय spike के परिणाम के बाद लें।

## संशोधित योजना: पहले V0, फिर न्यूनतम निजी assistant

### चरण 0 — एक सप्ताहांत का V0

**मकसद:** पता लगाना कि OpenClaw और आपके gateway से आपकी मुख्य जरूरत का कितना हिस्सा पहले से पूरा होता है।

- OpenClaw को बिना fork किए, isolated environment में चलाएँ।
- एक model gateway/provider configure करके सामान्य chat और tool calling जाँचें।
- Local UI से एक computer-use MCP/उपलब्ध CUA path जाँचें।
- केवल एक सुरक्षित desktop कार्य चलाएँ: Notepad खोलना, लिखना, सेव करना और परिणाम verify करना।
- अपने Windows setup पर screenshots, window focus, scaling और takeover को भी देखें।
- कोई bulk skill import या project rebrand अभी नहीं।

**निर्णय:** अगर chat, tools और computer action ठीक चलें, तो OpenClaw की मौजूदा UI से शुरुआत करें। अगर UI में ही आपकी जरूरत का बड़ा हिस्सा गायब हो, तभी नई UI का scaffold बनाएं।

### चरण 1 — Baseline और छोटा interface

- OpenClaw version/commit pin करें और upstream remote रखें।
- पहले Control UI को theme/config से अपने नाम के अनुरूप करें।
- एक personal assistant और दो specialists—जैसे Researcher और Computer Operator—बनाकर देखें।
- Model gateway से एक मजबूत model और जरूरत के अनुसार एक तेज़/कम लागत model route करें।
- Provider failover तभी जोड़ें जब gateway और चुने models में व्यवहार सत्यापित हो।

### चरण 2 — Windows computer control

- पहले isolated VM या अलग Windows account में काम करें।
- OpenClaw के experimental CUA path का अपना task set पर मूल्यांकन करें।
- UIA/accessible controls और screenshot/vision दोनों की जरूरत हो तो उसका छोटा spike करें; अभी अलग Python/C# service पहले से तय न करें।
- Live view, pause/stop और user takeover अनिवार्य रखें।
- तभी main desktop तक पहुँच पर विचार करें जब test cases और recovery तरीका स्पष्ट हों।

### चरण 3 — Skills और memory

- 3–5 जरूरी skills import करके उनकी dependencies और permissions जाँचें।
- अपनी corrections को Git/versioned files में रखें ताकि गलती पर वापस जा सकें।
- Skill Workshop प्रस्तावों को review करके apply करें; सीधे unrestricted self-editing चालू न करें।
- Memory को inspect/edit/delete करने योग्य रखें।
- Hermes patterns को तभी अपनाएँ जब कोई मापी हुई कमी हो—जैसे पुरानी chats ढूँढ़ना या skill evaluation।

### चरण 4 — Team workflow

- पहले OpenClaw subagents से Researcher → Writer जैसे सरल handoff चलाएँ।
- अगर task board, resumable jobs या budgets की कमी सामने आए, तभी छोटा `self-team` plugin/extension बनाएं।
- हर agent को सिर्फ़ अपने काम के tools दें। Computer Operator को web research और unrestricted shell एक साथ देना जरूरी नहीं।
- cancellation, timeouts, retry limits और daily API budget जोड़ें।

### चरण 5 — अपनी UI तभी बनाएं जब जरूरत साबित हो

OpenDots से Spaces, specialist cards और timeline की UX सीखें। पर अपनी UI तभी बनाएं जब OpenClaw Control UI आपके इस्तेमाल में सचमुच बाधा बने। नई UI को Gateway के supported protocol/API से जोड़ें; OpenDots का chat/storage runtime बिना निर्भरता जाँचे न उठाएँ।

### चरण 6 — Desktop app और हमेशा चालू रहना

आखिर में Electron shell, tray, quick-ask hotkey, startup behavior और backup/restore जोड़ें। Always-on routines पहले सीमित रखें; unattended computer actions को बाद में, logs और stop control के साथ सक्षम करें।

## निष्कर्ष

आपके तीन headline points में से **दो सही दिशा में हैं**: source merge से बचना और पहले V0 चलाना। तीसरा—“पूरा computer full access में दे देंगे, इसलिए permissions का issue नहीं”—मैं संशोधित करूँगा: multi-user permission UI हट सकती है, लेकिन **isolation, kill switch, authentication और destructive-action safeguards रहने चाहिए**।

अभी मेरा चुनाव होगा: **V0 चलाइए, Phase 0 scaffold नहीं।** V0 का परिणाम तय करेगा कि आपको वास्तव में नई UI, custom Windows computer service और team orchestrator में कितना काम करना पड़ेगा।

**हाँ, समझ गया।** आप चाहते हैं कि तीन AI developers—**DeepSeek Harness, Qoder और OpenCode**—एक ही workspace में काम करें, और मैं **ChatGPT के रूप में senior developer** बनकर उन्हें coordinate और उनके काम की समीक्षा करूँ।

मैं एक master plan, हर developer के लिए अलग निर्देश वाली file, और साझा `Agent Chat` log तैयार करूँ। आप किसी developer की report यहाँ लाएँगे; मैं उसका काम verify करके plan और coordination log अपडेट करूँगा।

एक सीमा साफ़ रखूँगा: मैं agents को लगातार background में चलाकर आपस में बात नहीं करवा सकता। वे साझा files पढ़कर और लिखकर coordinate कर सकते हैं; किसी की चुप्पी को अपने-आप काम पूरा होना नहीं मानूँगा।

### Developers को भेजने के लिए

हर assistant को उसका संदेश अलग भेजें:

```text
DeepSeek Harness, Start your task. Read E:\zero\DEVELOPER_DEEPSEEK_HARNESS.md and follow the next READY backend task. First read MASTER_PLAN.md, PROJECT_MEMORY.md, TASK_BOARD.md, DECISIONS.md, and the recent AGENT_CHAT.txt entries. Work in small, low-resource steps; update your report, board, and chat log; post a HANDOFF before stopping.
```

```text
Qoder, Start your task. Read E:\zero\DEVELOPER_QODER.md and follow the next READY computer-use task. First read MASTER_PLAN.md, PROJECT_MEMORY.md, TASK_BOARD.md, DECISIONS.md, and the recent AGENT_CHAT.txt entries. Work in small, low-resource steps; use only the designated Zero computer environment; update your report, board, and chat log; post a HANDOFF before stopping.
```

## Key findings
1. **CopilotKit is easy to preserve** — its usage is confined to just 3 client files (`App.tsx` provider, `Chat.tsx` run/stop hooks, `ThreadList.tsx` thread listing). ComputerPanel (live view + takeover), Spaces/pages editor, tasks, connections, theming, and the 75 KB design system are plain React, reusable for Zero after rebranding.
2. **Intelligence is the real lock-in** — all conversation messages/tool events persist *only* in CopilotKit Intelligence; local SQLite stores just thread bindings and metadata. The exact SDK runtime route contract (`runtime-scope.ts`: threads CRUD, agent run/stop, subscribe, messages) is now documented as the surface a Zero adapter must implement, backed by OpenClaw sessions.
3. **Bridge transports identified for UI-01** — OpenDots runs models via an OpenAI-compatible chat-completions adapter; OpenClaw offers the same shape (disabled by default) plus an OpenResponses SSE endpoint, and its WS protocol for session control. Three candidate transports mapped, pending coordination with DeepSeek Harness.
4. **OpenClaw Control UI verdict** — Solid 2, ~6.7k files, protocol-coupled to the Gateway. Satisfactory as an operator console/fallback; not the right base for the Zero product UX. Keep it; build Zero UI from OpenDots components + a small adapter.

## The findings that change the plan

- **The embedding contract is documented upstream.** `docs/gateway/embedding.md` tells a host app to supervise `openclaw` as a child process, use Gateway WebSocket RPC as the control plane, and *never* read or mutate OpenClaw's state files. Zero's adapter box now has a supported contract instead of an invented one.
- **One port serves both protocols** — WS RPC plus OpenAI-compatible HTTP (`/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/models`, `/v1/embeddings`). `model` is an *agent target* (`openclaw/default`, `openclaw/<agentId>`), and thread→session mapping works via a stable `user` string or `x-openclaw-session-key`. Caveats: it's stateless by default, and it's full operator access (loopback only).
- **Lead + Researcher + Operator is configuration, not code** — `agents.entries` + `bindings` + `subagents.allowAgents`, with per-agent model, workspace, skills, tools and sandbox.
- **Don't build a coordinator yet.** Sub-agents, Swarm fan-out, session goals with token budgets, `sessions_send`, and a bundled-but-disabled **Workboard** (full task lifecycle, dependency promotion, dispatch to agents) already cover the documented need. Nothing has failed acceptance — nothing has been tried.
- **`full` session permission mode** = unrestricted filesystem, no exec reviewer, requires `operator.admin`. That's the "no per-action approval" knob, with sandbox/workspace root as the real containment (aligns with D-003).
- **Two real gaps for us:** memory has no edit path (LanceDB is store/recall/forget only), and skills carry no license metadata and no version for git/local installs. Both are BE-03 affordance work, not new engines.

## Three asks blocking the next tickets

1. **BE-01** needs the gateway product/endpoint/auth style plus an approved way to place the key in the host secret store — I won't put a key in chat, a report, or a config file.
2. **BE-02** needs one announced, approved, non-overlapping window to activate pnpm 12.15.0 via corepack and install dependencies. That's the gate between "documented" and "proven".
3. Optional and cheap: approval to add `docs/concepts/` to the sparse checkout — it's where the multi-agent/parallel-specialist intent pages live, currently the biggest documentation blind spot.

## What I produced

**[mvp-backend.md](reports/backend/mvp-backend.md)** — the Lead/Researcher/Operator plan, all as configuration rather than code:

- **Agent roster:** `zero` (Lead) → `researcher` / `operator`. The specialists both get `subagents.allowAgents: []`, so "no runaway team" is one config key per agent, not application logic.
- **Proposed JSON5** with the reasons each non-obvious key exists — e.g. `profile: "coding"` + `alsoAllow: ["computer"]` for the Operator (the `coding` profile *excludes* `computer`), and `allow`/`alsoAllow` can never appear in the same scope because validation rejects it.
- **Provider shape** for a custom OpenAI-compatible gateway, plus the **8 checks that decide whether that block survives** — tool calling and vision are the two that would change the architecture if absent.
- **Event contract** for OpenCode's adapter, including the two traps that would otherwise cost a day each.

**[gateway-probe.mjs](reports/backend/tools/gateway-probe.mjs)** — BE-01 becomes one command. Self-tested before any endpoint existed: a throwaway loopback mock (started outside the repo, then deleted) produced **8 pass / 0 warn / 0 fail**, exit 0. Negative paths verified too — missing env → exit 2; unreachable port → five clean FAILs and exit 1 with no crash; the written JSON report grepped for the literal test key → **0 occurrences**.

## The coordination my teammates needed

**Qoder** asked where the computer tool lives and whether a Gateway-side hold is possible. Answer: it is not possible by configuration — upstream states outright *"There is no lease, expiry, or arm/disarm command"*. But Qoder's independent seam finding and my documented hook turned out to be **the same mechanism**: `registerTrustedToolPolicy` returns the same `PluginToolPolicyDecision` that `before_tool_call` does. So a small Zero plugin gives us the hold without patching upstream — with the crucial semantic that a human hold must `block`, never `requireApproval` (which pauses the *run* rather than refusing the action). I also told Qoder not to duplicate raw `node.invoke` enforcement — that's `gateway.nodes.commands.deny`, my lane.

**OpenCode** got the transport-neutral bridge contract: thread→session identity, the stateless-HTTP trap, one text projection per display, and refetch-on-reconnect.

## Also closed

[license-posture.md](reports/backend/license-posture.md) — the one unblocked in-lane item. Two findings worth flagging: **Hermes code is already adapted inside OpenClaw** (MIT, attributed), and the Windows CUA native package is **"MIT AND MPL-2.0"** while upstream's NOTICE file never mentions CUA — harmless locally, a real distribution obligation later.

## 4. `src/server`
Routes `computer-routes.ts:28-60`: `GET /dots/:id/computer`, `PATCH …/permissions`, `POST …/start|stop|take|release|actions`; mounted at `app.ts:93` under `/api`. Owner (human, via OWNER token) starts/stops/takes control (`computer-service.ts:254-268`, `269-323`); agent only acts through tools. Supervisor calls `/computers`, `/computers/{id}/ensure`, `/stop` (`:136-143,186-190,257,266`); computer HTTP paths built at `:359-361` (`/screenshot`, `/snapshot`, `/files/list`, `/exec`, `/human/click`…), container port 4100 / loopback port (`:160-183`), 4 MB response cap (`:103`), 70 s deadline (`:32`), secret redaction (`:109-116`), 50 ms permission re-check cancels in-flight (`:348-354`), 409 → "refresh snapshot" (`:88-91`). Tools: `computer-tools.ts:10-28` exposes `computer_<action>` (drops `human_*`) via `@copilotkit/runtime/v2` `defineTool`. Agent wiring/pause: `dot-agent.ts:169-173`, `:360-364`, prompt `:364`. Persistence: `computer-store.ts:9-10` SQLite tables `computer_permissions`, `computer_audit`; 1000-record trim `:40`, 50-row read `:47`. Config `platform-config.ts:60-63`. **Proprietary dependency**: conversations/threads need CopilotKit **Intelligence** (`platform.ts:6,31,50-53,126,155-171`; `README.md:17`) — so the agent loop is gated by it, while the computer runtime itself is self-hosted OpenBot.

## 5. Docs (claims, not code)
`docs/COMPUTERS.md:3` provider = OpenBot computer service + supervisor; `:9-16` prerequisites (Docker Engine + Compose v2 + BuildKit additional contexts, Node 24, two ≥24-char secrets); `:46` takeover/handback + fresh snapshot; `:51` no destructive reset exposed; `:53` cancellation may not stop upstream browser op; `:55` **limits**: shared host kernel, no egress policy, gVisor not installed by default; `:65` tests use fixtures only. `docs/demos/README.md:46-52` live checks passed; `:54` Slack/spoken delegation unverified. `docs/SETUP.md:127` computer tools replace read-only `read_public_page` when browser provider chosen (code: `dot-agent.ts:174-184`). `README.md:67,211`.

## 6. Event contract
No computer-specific event types exist. Only AG-UI generic `EventType` used for runs: `RUN_ERROR`, `RUN_FINISHED` (`dot-agent.ts:12,33,78,82,88,93,431-453`), `TEXT_MESSAGE_START/CONTENT` (`setup-telemetry.ts:266-286`). Computer signals ride as **tool calls** named `computer_*` with args from `computerInputs`, results as JSON passthrough, plus owner REST `ComputerStatus`.

## 7. Licensing
`LICENSE:1-3` MIT, Copyright (c) Atai Barkai; `package.json:6` `"license": "MIT"`, `"private": true`, `:3` description names "CopilotKit Intelligence"; `deployment/computers/LICENSE.openbot:1-3` MIT (c) 2026 CopilotKit. **No per-file license headers** on any computer file inspected.

## Absent
No screen-capture, no browser automation, no container-creation code in the checked-out paths — all delegated to the pinned OpenBot supervisor/computer image (`computer-service.ts:136-190,362`). No Windows/desktop-agent/RDP/VNC code; the runtime is a headless Linux Chromium container (`compose.computers.yml:27`). No mouse move/drag/right-click, no clipboard, no OS-level app launching or window management — only browser-scoped `click/type/key/scroll` plus in-container `exec`/files. No continuous video stream (polled PNG base64 only), no frame sequence numbers, no viewport/resolution control, no audio. `src/browser/*` (Playwright service), `tests/*`, `.github/*`, `public/*` exist upstream but are **not checked out** (`.git/info/sparse-checkout`, `git ls-tree HEAD`).

# OpenMausBot computer-use inventory (commit d393b7a, sparse checkout: docs, electron, server, src/components, src/lib)

## 1. Capability present
Full computer-use exists, but the driver itself is a **third-party closed binary**: `@trycua/cua-driver` v0.28.2 (`package.json:104`; per-platform packages `cua-driver-win32-x64-msvc` etc., `pnpm-lock.yaml:2186-2196`). The repo only hosts/licenses it. Tool surface per `docs/computer-use-integration.md:130-135`: click, double_click, right_click, drag, scroll, type_text, press_key, hotkey, move_cursor, get_window_state, get_desktop_state, **get_accessibility_tree**, list_windows, screenshot, zoom — UIA/AX-style element paths preferred over pixels (docs claim; implemented inside the driver, not this repo). Cloud-desktop tools are re-implemented in-repo via shell: `server/cloud-computer-tools.ts:14-26` (screenshot/click/drag/type_text/scroll via xdotool/xclip, lines 63-78).

## 2. Architecture / entry files
- **Electron main owns the daemon**: `electron/cua.mjs` — `startCua()` (247), `resolveDriverBinary()` (118), `startEmbedded()` (209) spawns `EmbeddedCuaDriverHost` from `@trycua/cua-driver/embedded`; IPC handlers `registerCuaIpc()` (394-436).
- Driver is exposed to agents as a **stdio MCP server** (`cua-driver mcp --embedded --socket <path>`, `cua.mjs:234`); the connection descriptor is written to `<userData>/cua-connection.json` (`electron/cua-connection.cjs:19-58`) and **the server harness reads/validates it** (`server/local-computer.ts:376-413`, validation 111-126, MCP protocol `2025-06-18` at :205). Docs: `docs/computer-use-integration.md:99-128`.
- Alternative providers: cloud "Boat" VMs (`server/boat.ts`, `server/cloud-computer-tools.ts`), containerized "Local VM" running Cua + noVNC (`server/container-computer.ts`), VPS (`server/vps-computer.ts`).
- Linux-only in-repo runtime supervisor: `electron/cua-linux-runtime.cjs` (912 lines), `cua-linux.cjs`, `cua-linux-bundle.cjs`.

## 3. Windows specifics
- Driver paths: `~/.cua-driver/packages/current/cua-driver.exe`, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\CuaDriver\cua-driver.exe`, packaged `Resources/cua-driver.exe` + console-less `cua-driver-background.exe` (`cua.mjs:42-45,121-137`); staged dev binary `dist-native/cua-win32-x64/cua-driver.exe` (`cua.mjs:126`, test `electron/cua-windows-isolation.test.mjs:97-103`). Packaging: `electron-builder.yml:163-169` (outside ASAR).
- Windows **must use the owned embedded host, never adopt a shared pipe** (`cua.mjs:176-178,287`; test lines 46-67); named pipes `\\.\pipe\...` (test:24). Ready only in `mode==="embedded"` (`electron/capabilities.cjs:67-69`). Elevation/UAC: no code; `chmod` no-ops noted (`cua-connection.cjs:29-31`). DPI/multi-monitor: **not handled in this repo** — `get_screen_size` returns `scale_factor` but only inside the driver (`docs/computer-use-integration.md:111`). Locked session: main treats `powerMonitor.getSystemIdleState(1)==="locked"` as `screenLocked` (`electron/main.mjs:1512`). No Windows capture code in-repo; `selectCaptureSource` has **no "win32" branch** (`electron/screen-preview.cjs:55-70`).

## 4. Observation
- Model side: screenshots arrive via MCP tool results; de-dup by sha256 of full frame + crop region (`server/computer-observation.ts:34-65`, `CropRegion` 15-20).
- Frames must never leak while human holds control: checked before *and* after capture with revision guard (`server/private-screen-capture.ts:4-14`), plus a cached-frame surface guard (`server/screen-frame-source.ts:22-70`).
- Live "Team map": JPEG thumbnails (480px, q60) captured via `scrot`/ImageMagick/`ffmpeg x11grab` inside the Linux VM (`server/routes/computer-frames.ts:12-14,74-79`; `server/boat.ts:1201-1203`), polled via `GET /api/computers/frames?have=ID:VERSION` (:84), token-bucket budget 4 fps (`server/computer-frames.ts:81`). PNG/JPEG mime conversion `private-screen-capture.ts:13`.

## 5. Takeover / stop
- **Take/release control**: `server/computer-control.ts` — in-memory `ComputerControl` with `take` (65), `release` (92), opaque `acquireLease/ownsLease/releaseLease` (73-107), `requestHelp` (110). While held, bot actions are **refused, never queued** (header comment 1-3). HTTP: `POST /api/bots/<id>/computer/control {action:"take"|"release", controlLeaseId}` (`server/computer-take-control.e2e.test.ts:155,168`; `server/routes/computers.ts:182,442`; `/api/team-computers/<id>/control` :189). Person-away timeout text `server/computer-wait.ts:60-63`.
- **UI**: `src/components/computer-takeover.ts` — `useComputerTakeover` (85) with `take/handBack/leave`, auto-release on `pagehide` (139-141), lease released if tab closes (20-26, 62); `openProviderDesktop` (30). Live drivable panel: `src/components/LiveDesktop.tsx:11-12,125-127` ("Hand back" button), `src/components/ComputerPanel.tsx:665,906-921`. Real desktop = noVNC RFB over `/api/desktop-viewer/<target>/websockify` (`src/lib/desktop-connection.ts:1-74`), view_only promoted/demoted fail-closed (`electron/desktop-workspace.cjs:10-17,138,226`).
- **Global stop**: no single kill-switch; per-turn `POST /api/bots/<id>/interrupt` (`server/routes/bot-messages.ts:632-688`, `stoppedByPerson`), Local VM `run|start|stop|remove` endpoints (`server/routes/computers.ts:347-407`), tray only Open/Quit (`electron/system-tray.mjs:17-21`), and `stopCua()` kills the embedded host (`cua.mjs:324-348`).

## 6. Safety/permissions
Never-approve shell floor rules incl. Windows folders (`server/safety-floor.ts:1-30`); per-bot command allowlist store (`server/command-allowlist.ts`); approval modes and cards (`src/components/ApprovalCard.tsx`, `ApprovalModeSelector.tsx`, `docs/approval-levels.md`); tool policy `server/agent-tool-policy.ts`; local-only IPC gating (`electron/local-origin.cjs` via `cua.mjs:395`); desktop viewers HTTPS-or-loopback-only, no credentials in URL (`electron/desktop-viewer.cjs:17-24`); display-media capture gated by one-shot intent + origin + user-gesture (`electron/screen-preview.cjs:14-53`); macOS Accessibility/Screen-Recording enforced pre-start (`cua.mjs:214-222`); Wayland/headless Linux refused (`capabilities.cjs:38-53`).

## 7. Licensing
Root: **Apache-2.0** (`LICENSE`, `package.json:15`), relicensed from MIT (`NOTICE:4-5`). Carve-out: `enterprise/` under proprietary "OpenMausBot Enterprise License" (`LICENSING.md:5-10`) — **not in this checkout**. Trademarks of Supamaus Software Private Limited; Apache grants no trademark rights (`LICENSING.md:41-46`). No SPDX per-file headers found in inspected files. Driver dependency `@trycua/cua-driver` license: no NOTICE/third_party entry in checkout (`NOTICE` has no trycua mention; `third_party/` not expanded) — must verify externally (trycua components are commonly MIT, binary unconfirmed).

## 8. Reuse candidates
`server/computer-control.ts` (concise lease/refuse-while-held semantics); `server/private-screen-capture.ts` + `server/screen-frame-source.ts` (hold-aware capture gating); `server/computer-observation.ts` (hash-dedup frame sending); `electron/desktop-viewer.cjs` + `electron/desktop-workspace.cjs` (viewer URL hardening, view_only demotion fail-closed); `src/components/computer-takeover.ts` + `LiveDesktop.tsx` + `src/lib/desktop-connection.ts` (takeover/live-view UI + noVNC wiring); `electron/screen-preview.cjs` (display-media intent guard); `server/computer-frames.ts` (budgeted multi-machine thumbnail watcher); `electron/cua.mjs` (embedded-driver lifecycle + Windows isolation as a pattern).

## 9. Desktop-session dependency
Local control **requires Electron main + a running interactive desktop**: the driver daemon spawns only from Electron main (`docs/computer-use-integration.md:64-75`); connection state persisted to `cua-connection.json` read by the server (`server/local-computer.ts:376`); Linux needs an Xorg session (`electron/capabilities.cjs:50`, "headless-session"). Cloud Boat paths assume X11 inside a Linux container (`server/boat.ts:51,1198-1203`) — none of that is Windows-native. An API-only/headless backend would lose all local capture/input.

## NOT in this sparse checkout
The driver itself (`cua-driver.exe`, `cua-sdk` native libs — binary, imported as `@trycua/cua-driver`, `cua.mjs:157-174`); `shared/` modules imported everywhere but not expanded (`server/computer-control.ts:5`, `screen-frame-source.ts` deps); `third_party/`, `enterprise/`, `apps/`, `cloudflare/`; all Windows input/UIA logic (lives in the external driver); `computer-proxy-local.ts` named in `docs/computer-use-integration.md:19` does not exist on disk (closest: `server/local-computer.ts`, `server/proxy-paths.ts`); no pywinauto/Win32-API/MJPEG/WebRTC desktop code — WebRTC appears only for live voice calls (`server/routes/live.ts:61`).

Two things worth your attention
The preflight caught a bug in its own check. config.computerScope failed because researcher denies "computer" and my substring match read the deny as a grant. Fixed to compare grant lists. The config was right; the checker was wrong — which is exactly what the check is for.

I recommend gating on Phase 1, not the install. The model-gateway probe needs no install and answers the one question that can invalidate everything: does your gateway support tool calling and vision? If tools fails, the Lead can't spawn specialists and the Operator can't use the computer tool — and we shouldn't spend 3 GB discovering that. Phase 1 is 5 minutes; Phase 2 is 2.5–4 GB and 5–15 minutes, one job, no overlap.

It's written against cited source contracts (host-hooks.ts:69-84, plugin-api.types.ts:271-283/412, response-types.ts:4-9, error-shape.ts:6-12) and imports nothing, so it carries no module-resolution risk before the install exists. The one that mattered: C6′
Qoder's finding was real and I'd left it as a note rather than a fix: code-mode invokes the computer tool through a {code} envelope with no action field, so a policy matching only computer let those calls through allowed and uncounted — a fail-open hole directly behind D-009's "nothing acts while I drive" promise.

Fixed in the plugin: exec is now in the matcher, and anything with toolKind: "code_mode_exec" is treated as opaque input — refused during a hold, and when not held allowed but counted against the budget and audited. "Allowed and uncounted" is gone either way.

This is defence in depth, not a substitute for Qoder's O7. Turning code-mode off for the Operator is still @manager's call and still the stronger answer — mine is the belt, theirs is the braces.

- wait clears staleness — Qoder confirmed my get_window_state reasoning against computer-use.md:92 and went further: wait also returns a desktop capture, so it qualifies. Adopted; get_window_state still only with includeScreenshot: true.
- Activation self-check — Qoder caught that my "registered policy=…" log would print even when the host silently rejected the policy. That's worse than no log. register() now reads its own manifest and logs a loud error if the policy id isn't declared, a warning if the manifest is unreadable, and never throws.
- O8 — blockedActions: ["escalate_scope"] now ships in the deployed template config while the plugin's code default stays neutral. I agree with Qoder's recommendation but it's a product-safety call, so it's a visible, strikeable config line rather than a hidden default.

planner
**CopilotKit** एक open-source toolkit है जिससे AI assistant का chat interface और app से उसका connection बनाया जाता है। Zero में यह frontend chat UI और runtime adapter के लिए इस्तेमाल हो रहा है: आपकी लिखी बात ZeroAgent तक जाती है, जो उसे OpenClaw Gateway और चुने हुए model तक पहुँचाता है।

## Current slice complete

### What changed

- **Auth gate removed from the live experience.** The screen was the upstream Control UI login gate served by the **pre-restart** Gateway process. The live config already has `gateway.bind: "loopback"` and `gateway.auth.mode: "none"`; after the 07:49 restart the gate is gone. No upstream or backend auth code was changed.
- **Removed dead Zero-owned auth CSS.** Deleted the unused `.unlock` rules from `app/src/client/style.css`; no TSX/JS references remained.
- **Added computer control to the native Zero Team page.**
  - `app/zero-control-ui/browser.js`: `Computer control` card backed only by the existing `zero.computer.control` method.
  - Status, Take control, Hand back, global-hold labeling, owned/other-holder states, release on `pagehide`/`dispose`, and a link to the built-in Desktop page at `/systems`.
  - `app/zero-control-ui/index.css`: card/action styles.
  - `app/zero-control-ui/test/mount.test.mjs`: fake hold state machine; take/release and dispose-release assertions.

### Risks / open items

1. **Stale hold on abnormal disconnect.** The hold is in-memory and owner-scoped. `pagehide`/`dispose` release it, but a killed tab that fires neither could leave agent input refused until restart. Qoder needs to decide on disconnect cleanup or a documented recovery.
2. **Real refusal proof remains CU-01.** The live card exercised the hold state machine, not a real agent desktop action. The trusted policy must still be proven against a live hold on the isolated environment.
3. **Desktop input not configured.** `/systems` currently shows “Set up a desktop server”; actual viewing/input needs CU-01.
4. **CLI plugin reload still fails** with the Windows symlink `EPERM` in `.openclaw/tmp/plugin-captures`. The Control UI `plugins.controlUi.reload` path worked. Use that path in this workspace until the loader issue is repaired.
5. **Memory scope.** `memory.search` works for memory files by default; session transcripts are not searched without an explicit config decision. Do not render session-only empty results as “no memories”.
6. **Screenshot limitation.** The browser screenshot tool required a visible desktop window and could not capture; verification above is live DOM/text evidence.

{
  "designated": true,
  "designatedBy": "owner",
  "ticket": "CU-01",
  "o1Resolved": true,
  "holdRecoveryApproved": true,
  "environmentLabel": "zero-cu01-isolated",
  "recordedAtUtc": "2026-10-23T03:30:00Z"
}
Field rules ([k7-run.mjs (line 59)](file:///E:/zero/computer/spike/k7-run.mjs#L59))
Field	Rule
designated	बिल्कुल true (boolean)
designatedBy	बिल्कुल "owner" — agent self-designate नहीं कर सकता
ticket	बिल्कुल "CU-01"
o1Resolved	बिल्कुल true
holdRecoveryApproved	बिल्कुल true — state-mutating take के लिए अनिवार्य
environmentLabel	non-empty string; role label, account name नहीं
recordedAtUtc	ISO-8601 UTC, Z पर खत्म; parseable


- Strings की जगह booleans नहीं चलेंगे (जैसे "true" reject होगा)।
- Extra fields allowed हैं, पर minimal रखना बेहतर है।
- environmentLabel की value उस box की भूमिका बताती है (designated isolated environment), आपका नाम/email नहीं।
Marker लिखने के बाद live run से पहले सिर्फ़ यह check (कोई Gateway call/desktop input नहीं)
node computer/spike/k7-run.mjs --plan --marker computer/spike/designated-environment.json

owner
तुम पुराना कॉन्टेक्स भूल जाते हो, जो हमारी बातचीत है वो भूल जाते हो। तो मैं चाहता हूँ कि तुम परानी चीज जो हम बनाने का ट्राय कर रहे हैं, तुम्हारे पास पुराने चैट सारी है, कॉन्टेक्ट साथ करो और चीजों को बनाओ। और तुम्हें खुद काम नहीं करना है, तुम्हें सिर्फ डेवलपर से काम करवाना है। ये चीज मेमोरी में अपग्रेड होनी चाहिए तुम्हारे कि तुम्हें खुद काम नहीं करना है, डेवलपर से करवाना है। और हमारे पहले एक डीप सीकर के डेवलपर था, जो छुट्टी पे है, तो उसकी जगह जेमिनाई करके एक डेवलपर आया है, तो उसके लिए प्रॉम्प्ट दो। बाकी हमारे दो डेवलपर अभी भी काम कर रहे हैं Open Code और CodeEd, वो काम कर रहे हैं, तो उनके लिए काम बताओ, पुराने कॉन्टेक्ट को याद रख के। तुम्हें खुद काम नहीं करना है बिल्कुल भी, तुम्हें बस तुम सीनियर डेवलपर हो, वो लोग नॉर्मल डेवलपर हैं, तो उनकी काम पर तुम्हें नज़र रखनी है बस।

owner
Aapke **OpenClaw** (jise aapne Open-Falcon kaha) project ke purane default gateway (`zero-gw`) ko completely disable karke **Kilo Code** jaisa Dynamic Custom Provider, Auto-Model Discovery (`84 models found`), Edit/Disconnect management aur Offline `.GGUF` model support banane ka complete research doc, architecture roadmap aur full source code niche diya gaya hai.

---

## Phase 1: Purane Default Gateway (`zero-gw`) ko Completely Disable Karna

Abhi aapke system (`DESKTOP-M24DI0N`, `Node v24.22.1`) par OpenClaw `ws://127.0.0.1:18789` ke zariye default `zero-gw` provider aur `ZERO_GATEWAY_KEY` environment variable use kar raha hai. Isko band karne ke liye ye steps follow karein:

1. **UI se Key Hatao:** Models tab ke andar `zero-gw` card me sabse niche laal rang ke **`Remove key`** button par click karein.


2. **Environment Variable Hatao:** Apne project ke `.env` file (ya Windows System Environment Variables) me jaakar `ZERO_GATEWAY_KEY` wali line ko delete ya comment (`#`) kar dein.


3. **Config File me Default Provider Disable Karein:** OpenClaw ke config folder (`~/.openclaw/config.json` ya project root ke `config/default.json`) me `zero-gw` ko disable karein:



```json
{
  "gateway": {
    "legacyZeroGateway": false,
    "defaultSession": "agent:zero:main",
    "dynamicProvidersEnabled": true
  },
  "providers": []
}

```

---

## Phase 2: Deep Research & Architecture (Ye Feature Kaise Kaam Karta Hai?)

Jo screenshots aapne share kiye hain (Kilo Code / modern AI Gateway UI ke), unke piche **4 core technical mechanisms** kaam karte hain:

### 1. Auto-Discovery & Live Connection Test (`Fetch models`)

* Jab aap `Provider API` me `OpenAI Compatible` select karke `Base URL` (jaise `[https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1](https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1)`) aur `API key` dalte hain, to frontend/backend turant us URL ke `/models` endpoint (`GET [https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/models](https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/models)`) par request bhejta hai.


* Agar URL aur API key sahi hain, to server `200 OK` ke saath saare models ki JSON list return karta hai (jaise aapke screenshot me **`84 models found`** aaya jisme `01-ai/yi-large`, `adept/fuyu-8b`, `deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct` wagaira dikh rahe hain).


* **Double Benefit:** Isse alag se "Test Connection" button dabane ki zaroorat nahi padti; models ki list aa gayi matlab API key aur Base URL 100% sahi kaam kar rahe hain.



### 2. Custom Provider & Capabilities Mapping (`Reasoning` & `Image`)

* Har model ke alag features hote hain, isliye UI me `ID` aur `Name` ke saath **`Reasoning`** (thinking models jaise DeepSeek-R1/o1) aur **`Image`** (vision input models) ke checkboxes aur bulk toggles (`Toggle reasoning for all`, `Toggle image for all`) diye gaye hain.


* Kuch providers ko API key ke alawa extra headers (jaise OpenRouter ya Cloudflare Gateway) chahiye hote hain, jiske liye niche **`Headers (optional)`** (`Header-Name` aur `value`) diya gaya hai.



### 3. Provider Edit, `Change API key` & `Disconnect` (CRUD Lifecycle)

* Ek baar **`Submit`** karne par provider database (`providers.json` ya SQLite) me `Provider ID` (jaise `myprovider` ya `Nvidia`) ke naam se save ho jata hai.


* Baad me **`Connected providers`** list me aapko **`Change API key`**, **`Edit`**, aur **`Disconnect`** ke buttons milte hain, jisse bina naya provider banaye aap purane provider ki key badal sakte hain ya naye models add/remove kar sakte hain.



### 4. Offline Local Models (`Import GGUF`)

* Aapke pehle screenshot me **`+ Add model`** -> **`Import GGUF`** ka option hai jo local offline models chalane ke kaam aata hai.


* Aapke PC me `Intel(R) Core(TM) i9-14900K` (32 cores) aur `32 GB RAM` hai, jo `.gguf` models ko local chalane ke liye kafi powerful hai.


* Backend me **`node-llama-cpp`** (ya embedded `llama-server`) use karke `.gguf` file ko load kiya jata hai aur use bhi local OpenAI-compatible stream ke roop me agent se jod diya jata hai.

```json

      "id": "nvidia",
      "displayName": "Nvidia",
      "providerApi": "openai-compatible",
```

---

### 2. Backend Gateway Service (`gateway-provider-routes.js` - Node.js)

Ye backend code aapke OpenClaw server me `/models` auto-fetch karega, provider save/edit/disconnect karega, aur `.gguf` import handle karega:

```javascript
// gateway-provider-routes.js (Node v24+ native fetch support ke saath)
import express from 'express';
import fs from 'fs/promises';
import path from 'path';

const router = express.Router();
const CONFIG_PATH = path.resolve('./config/providers-config.json');

// Helper: Config read aur write karne ke liye
async function readConfig() {
  try {
    const data = await fs.readFile(CONFIG_PATH, 'utf-8');
    return JSON.parse(data);
  } catch {
    return { customProviders: [], localGgufModels: [] };
  }
}

async function writeConfig(config) {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG_PATH), { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(CONFIG_PATH, JSON.stringify(config, null, 2), 'utf-8');
}

// 1. AUTO-DETECT / FETCH MODELS API (Base URL + API Key dalte hi models layega)
router.post('/api/providers/fetch-models', async (req, res) => {
  const { baseUrl, apiKey, headers = {} } = req.body;

  if (!baseUrl) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Base URL zaroori hai' });
  }

  try {
    const cleanUrl = baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
    const reqHeaders = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...headers
    };

    if (apiKey && apiKey.trim() !== '') {
      reqHeaders['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${apiKey.trim()}`;
    }

    // Provider ke /models endpoint par call
    const response = await fetch(`${cleanUrl}/models`, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: reqHeaders,
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000)
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errText = await response.text();
      return res.status(response.status).json({
        error: `Provider connection failed (${response.status}): ${errText}`
      });
    }

    const result = await response.json();
    const rawModels = Array.isArray(result.data) ? result.data : (Array.isArray(result) ? result : []);

    const formattedModels = rawModels.map((m) => ({
      id: m.id || m.name,
      name: m.name || m.id,
      reasoning: Boolean(m.reasoning || /r1|thinking|o1|o3/i.test(m.id)),
      image: Boolean(m.vision || /vision|vl|fuyu|llava/i.test(m.id))
    }));

    return res.json({
      success: true,
      count: formattedModels.length,
      models: formattedModels
    });
  } catch (err) {
    return res.status(500).json({ error: `Connection error: ${err.message}` });
  }
});

// 2. SAVE YA EDIT CUSTOM PROVIDER (Create + Update donon ke liye)
router.post('/api/providers/save', async (req, res) => {
  const { id, displayName, providerApi, baseUrl, apiKey, headers, models } = req.body;

  if (!/^[a-z0-9-_]+$/.test(id)) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      error: 'Provider ID me sirf lowercase letters, numbers, hyphens ya underscores allowed hain.'
    });
  }

  const config = await readConfig();
  const existingIndex = config.customProviders.findIndex((p) => p.id === id);

  const providerPayload = {
    id,
    displayName: displayName || id,
    providerApi: providerApi || 'OpenAI Compatible',
    baseUrl: baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, ''),
    // Agar edit karte waqt naya apiKey nahi bheja, to purana retain karein
    apiKey: apiKey !== undefined ? apiKey : (existingIndex >= 0 ? config.customProviders[existingIndex].apiKey : ''),
    headers: headers || {},
    models: models || [],
    status: 'connected',
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    config.customProviders[existingIndex] = providerPayload;
  } else {
    config.customProviders.push(providerPayload);
  }

  await writeConfig(config);
  res.json({ success: true, provider: providerPayload });
});

// 3. CHANGE API KEY QUICK ENDPOINT
router.patch('/api/providers/:id/api-key', async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const { apiKey } = req.body;
  const config = await readConfig();

  const provider = config.customProviders.find((p) => p.id === id);
  if (!provider) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Provider nahi mila' });

  provider.apiKey = apiKey;
  provider.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  await writeConfig(config);

  res.json({ success: true, message: 'API key update ho gayi' });
});

// 4. DISCONNECT / DELETE PROVIDER
router.delete('/api/providers/:id', async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const config = await readConfig();
  config.customProviders = config.customProviders.filter((p) => p.id !== id);
  await writeConfig(config);
  res.json({ success: true });
});

// 5. IMPORT OFFLINE GGUF MODEL
router.post('/api/models/import-gguf', async (req, res) => {
  const { filePath, displayName } = req.body;
  if (!filePath || !filePath.endsWith('.gguf')) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid .gguf file path dein' });
  }

  const config = await readConfig();
  const modelEntry = {
    id: `gguf-${Date.now()}`,
    name: displayName || path.basename(filePath, '.gguf'),
    filePath,
    importedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  config.localGgufModels.push(modelEntry);
  await writeConfig(config);
  res.json({ success: true, model: modelEntry });
});

export default router;

```

---

### 3. Frontend UI Code (`CustomProviderModal.jsx` - React + Tailwind CSS)

Ye component bilkul aapke screenshots jaisa UI banata hai: `Provider ID`, `Display name`, `Base URL` dalte hi auto-detect (`84 models found`), `Select all` / `Deselect all`, `Search models...`, `Reasoning` & `Image` toggles, `Headers (optional)` aur `Edit Provider` mode:

```jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function CustomProviderModal({ existingProvider = null, onClose, onSaved }) {
  const [providerId, setProviderId] = useState(existingProvider?.id || '');
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState(existingProvider?.displayName || '');
  const [providerApi, setProviderApi] = useState(existingProvider?.providerApi || 'OpenAI Compatible');
  const [baseUrl, setBaseUrl] = useState(existingProvider?.baseUrl || '');
  const [apiKey, setApiKey] = useState(existingProvider?.apiKey || '');

  // Added models list (manual + imported from fetched)
  const [models, setModels] = useState(existingProvider?.models || [
    { id: '', name: '', reasoning: false, image: false }
  ]);

  // Auto-discovered models state
  const [fetchedModels, setFetchedModels] = useState([]);
  const [selectedFetchedIds, setSelectedFetchedIds] = useState(new Set());
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [loadingModels, setLoadingModels] = useState(false);
  const [fetchError, setFetchError] = useState('');

  // Optional Headers state
  const [headers, setHeaders] = useState(
    existingProvider?.headers
      ? Object.entries(existingProvider.headers).map(([key, value]) => ({ key, value }))
      : [{ key: '', value: '' }]
  );

  // Base URL aur API key dalte hi auto-fetch (Debounced 800ms)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (baseUrl.startsWith('http') && apiKey.length > 5) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        handleFetchModels();
      }, 800);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [baseUrl, apiKey]);

  const handleFetchModels = async () => {
    setLoadingModels(true);
    setFetchError('');
    try {
      const headerObj = {};
      headers.forEach((h) => {
        if (h.key.trim()) headerObj[h.key.trim()] = h.value;
      });

      const res = await fetch('/api/providers/fetch-models', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ baseUrl, apiKey, headers: headerObj })
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to fetch models');

      setFetchedModels(data.models || []);
      // Default select all discovered models
      setSelectedFetchedIds(new Set((data.models || []).map((m) => m.id)));
    } catch (err) {
      setFetchError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoadingModels(false);
    }
  };

  const filteredFetchedModels = fetchedModels.filter((m) =>
    m.id.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  const toggleSelectAll = () => {
    setSelectedFetchedIds(new Set(filteredFetchedModels.map((m) => m.id)));
  };

  const toggleDeselectAll = () => {
    setSelectedFetchedIds(new Set());
  };

  const handleAddSelectedModels = () => {
    const picked = fetchedModels.filter((m) => selectedFetchedIds.has(m.id));
    // Existing empty manual row hata kar selected models merge karein
    const cleanExisting = models.filter((m) => m.id.trim() !== '');
    const mergedMap = new Map();
    [...cleanExisting, ...picked].forEach((m) => mergedMap.set(m.id, m));
    setModels(Array.from(mergedMap.values()));
    setFetchedModels([]);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    const headerObj = {};
    headers.forEach((h) => {
      if (h.key.trim()) headerObj[h.key.trim()] = h.value;
    });

    const validModels = models.filter((m) => m.id.trim() !== '');

    const res = await fetch('/api/providers/save', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        id: providerId,
        displayName,
        providerApi,
        baseUrl,
        apiKey,
        headers: headerObj,
        models: validModels
      })
    });

    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.success) {
      onSaved(data.provider);
      onClose();
    } else {
      alert(data.error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#141414] text-gray-200 p-6 rounded-xl max-w-2xl w-full border border-neutral-800 space-y-5">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
          {existingProvider ? `Edit Provider: ${existingProvider.displayName}` : 'Custom provider'}
        </h2>
        <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white">✕</button>
      </div>

      {/* Provider ID */}
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm mb-1">Provider ID</label>
        <input
          value={providerId}
          disabled={Boolean(existingProvider)}
          onChange={(e) => setProviderId(e.target.value.toLowerCase())}
          placeholder="myprovider"
          className="w-full bg-[#1c1c1c] border border-neutral-700 rounded p-2 text-sm"
        />
        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Lowercase letters, numbers, hyphens, or underscores</p>
      </div>

      {/* Display Name */}
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm mb-1">Display name</label>
        <input
          value={displayName}
          onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="My AI Provider"
          className="w-full bg-[#1c1c1c] border border-neutral-700 rounded p-2 text-sm"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Provider API */}
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm mb-1">Provider API</label>
        <select
          value={providerApi}
          onChange={(e) => setProviderApi(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full bg-[#1c1c1c] border border-neutral-700 rounded p-2 text-sm"
        >
          <option value="OpenAI Compatible">OpenAI Compatible</option>
          <option value="Anthropic Compatible">Anthropic Compatible</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* Base URL */}
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm mb-1">Base URL</label>
        <input
          value={baseUrl}
          onChange={(e) => setBaseUrl(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1"
          className="w-full bg-[#1c1c1c] border border-neutral-700 rounded p-2 text-sm"
        />
      </div>

      {/* API Key */}
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm mb-1">API key</label>
        <input
          type="password"
          value={apiKey}
          onChange={(e) => setApiKey(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="API key"
          className="w-full bg-[#1c1c1c] border border-neutral-700 rounded p-2 text-sm"
        />
        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Optional. Leave empty if you manage auth via headers.</p>
      </div>

      {/* Models Section Header */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-2">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Models</span>
        <div className="flex gap-4 text-xs text-gray-300">
          <button onClick={handleFetchModels} className="hover:text-white">
            ↵ {loadingModels ? 'Fetching...' : 'Fetch models'}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setModels(models.map((m) => ({ ...m, reasoning: !m.reasoning })))}
            className="hover:text-white"
          >
            Toggle reasoning for all
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setModels(models.map((m) => ({ ...m, image: !m.image })))}
            className="hover:text-white"
          >
            Toggle image for all
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {fetchError && <p className="text-xs text-red-400">{fetchError}</p>}

      {/* Auto-Discovered Models Box (84 models found style) */}
      {fetchedModels.length > 0 && (
        <div className="border border-neutral-800 bg-[#181818] rounded-lg p-4 space-y-3">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
            <span className="font-semibold">{fetchedModels.length} models found</span>
            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <button onClick={toggleSelectAll} className="hover:underline">Select all</button>
              <button onClick={toggleDeselectAll} className="hover:underline">Deselect all</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <input
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search models..."
            className="w-full bg-[#121212] border border-neutral-700 rounded p-2 text-xs"
          />

          <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 pr-1">
            {filteredFetchedModels.map((m) => (
              <label key={m.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={selectedFetchedIds.has(m.id)}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const next = new Set(selectedFetchedIds);
                    e.target.checked ? next.add(m.id) : next.delete(m.id);
                    setSelectedFetchedIds(next);
                  }}
                />
                <span>{m.id}</span>
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
            <button
              onClick={handleAddSelectedModels}
              className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded"
            >
              Add {selectedFetchedIds.size} model(s)
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setFetchedModels([])}
              className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Submit Button */}
      <button
        onClick={handleSubmit}
        className="w-full bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-medium py-2 rounded text-sm"
      >
        Submit
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

```

---

## Phase 4: Offline `.GGUF` Models Kaise Chalayein (`node-llama-cpp`)

Jab aap UI me **`+ Add model`** -> **`Import GGUF`** par click karke apne PC (`DISK C:\` ya `DISK E:\` jahan `398 GB free` hai) se koi `.gguf` file select karenge, to use OpenClaw ke andar bina internet ke chalane ke liye ye package install karein:

```bash
npm install node-llama-cpp

```

Aur apne OpenClaw model runner me ye wrapper add karein:

```javascript
import { getLlama, LlamaChatSession } from 'node-llama-cpp';

export async function runLocalGgufPrompt(ggufFilePath, userPrompt) {
  const llama = await getLlama();
  const model = await llama.loadModel({ modelPath: ggufFilePath });
  const context = await model.createContext();
  const session = new LlamaChatSession({ contextSequence: context.getSequence() });

  const response = await session.prompt(userPrompt);
  return response;
}

```

## Phase 5: Step-by-Step Implementation Roadmap

| Step | Task | File / Location | Goal |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Step 1** | Legacy `zero-gw` Disable Karein | `.env` & UI `Remove key` button

 | Purana single-provider gateway hatana.

 |
| **Step 2** | Backend Provider Routes Jodna | `src/gateway/gateway-provider-routes.js` | `/fetch-models`, `/save`, `/api-key`, aur `/import-gguf` endpoints banana.

 |
| **Step 3** | Custom Provider UI Lagana | `src/ui/components/CustomProviderModal.jsx` | `Base URL` + `API Key` dalte hi auto-detect (`84 models found`) aur bulk add karna.

 |
| **Step 4** | Connected Providers List Banana | `src/ui/pages/ModelsPage.jsx` | Connected providers (`Nvidia` etc.) ke samne `Edit`, `Change API key`, aur `Disconnect` buttons dena.

 |
| **Step 5** | Local GGUF Loader Enable Karna | `src/runtime/gguf-runner.js` | `+ Add model` -> `Import GGUF` se local offline models chalana.

 |

Kya aap chahte hain ki main aapke local OpenClaw repo ke exact folder structure ke hisaab se batayu ki `gateway-provider-routes.js` aur `CustomProviderModal.jsx` ko kis file me import karna hai?

Aur fir main dekho tumhe kuch photos aur provide kar raha hu uske andar dekho model dekhenge aur input GGP-UFO form wala bhi hai usko matlab input offline model input karke bhi chala sakte hai. Aur dusre photo me tum dekhoge to custom provider ka option hai to main chahta hu na ki iske andar na custom provider ka option dal jaye. Custom provider dal jaye jisse main kya custom provider dal sakta hu koi bhi. Us customer provider ke andar maine click kiya to dekho usme dekhoge likha hua hai confirmed custom provider seen in progress after docs. Provider ID, display name, provider API, base URL, API key. Aur dusre photo me dekhoge to usi ko scroll karunga to maine API dali aur URL dala. Sirf API aur URL dalte hi na usne detect kar liya total kitne model hai isme 84 model hai jo maine photo bheja usme 84 model dikh raha hai. Usme upar dekhoge right side me waha pe select all, deselect all, search ka button hai fir waha pe tick karunga fir add models ko karunga to wo models add ho jayenge fir submit kar denge wo custom provider add chalu ho jayega. Dusre photo me dekhoge sirf URL aur API dali hui hai utne me hi isne detect kar liya hai ki aur detect karne ka matlab ye bhi hota hai ki wo sahi se kaam kar raha hai. To mujhe na apne Open-Falcon ke andar jo pehle se iska gateway feature hai usko disable karwa ke ye wale se ye wala usme add karwana hai is type se banwana chahta hu jisse providers isme add multiple providers add karna mere liye aasan ho jayega. Aur fir main tumhe ek aur photo deta हूं iske andar dekh lo edit provider ka aa raha hai to baad me bhi fir iske andar aur bhi chize kar sakte hai matlab confirm provider banne ke baad ye isme edit wagara kar sakta hu दोबारा दोबारा bhi matlab banne ke baad bhi naya nahi banana padta. Is type ka gateway feature banana chahta hu. To iske liye complete plan bana ke do mujhe ek achha sa. मुझे एक कंप्लीट रोडमैप चाहिए। एक-एक चीज़ें बारीकी से, डिटेल्स, कैसे बनते हैं उसके ऊपर डॉक्स, रिसर्च जितनी भी बनी हुई है सब कुछ चाहिए। कैसे बनाते हैं, कैसे बनता है, कैसे यूज़ होता है, सब कुछ चाहिए। और अगर तुम्हें इसका सोर्स कोड मिल जाए, मैं जो यूज़ कर रहा हूं वो किलो कोड के फोटो दिए हैं मैंने। किलो कोड के अंदर का उसके प्रोवाइडर का फोटो दिया। अगर तुम्हें इसका सोर्स कोड भी मिलता है कहीं पे ये गेटवे बनाने के तो वो भी लेके आना मेरे लिए। पूरे अच्छे से रिसर्च करो। spawn cheaper worker subagents to save on the costs मैंने तुमको अपनी एडवाइस बता दिया है। अब तुम अपने हिसाब से देख लो कि कैसे ये करते हैं, कैसे ठीक होगा, कैसे नहीं होगा, अपने प्रोजेक्ट के अंदर कैसे ऐड कर सकते हैं। मैंने फोटो भी प्रोवाइड कर दिया है। देख लो अब तुम।

planner
हाँ, screenshots Kilo Code के **Custom Provider Manager** flow के हैं। Research और complete roadmap मैंने [zero-provider-manager-roadmap.md](E:\zero\plans\zero-provider-manager-roadmap.md) में रख दिया है।

## जरूरी decision

**OpenClaw Gateway बंद नहीं करेंगे।** वही Zero का runtime है: agents, sessions, memory, tools और native UI उसी से चलते हैं।

बंद/replace होगा केवल वर्तमान fixed provider **`zero-gw`**। नया custom provider सफल test और activation के बाद ही उसे हटाएँगे, ताकि Zero बीच में बिना model के बंद न हो।

## जो बनेगा

- Native Zero Control UI में **Providers** page
- Add/Edit/Delete/Disable provider
- Provider ID, display name, API type, Base URL, API key, optional headers
- OpenAI-compatible endpoint से `/v1/models` model discovery
- Search, Select All, Deselect All, manual model add
- Multiple providers और per-agent model selection
- Test connection before activation
- Activate करने से पहले role/model diff और failure पर rollback
- API key UI से enter होगी, पर config/log/UI response में कभी वापस नहीं दिखेगी
- पहले existing local endpoint: Ollama, LM Studio, llama.cpp, vLLM, SGLang
- बाद में अलग **Import GGUF** flow: local model file, RAM/VRAM check, managed loopback llama.cpp server

84 models दिखना केवल यह prove करता है कि NVIDIA endpoint का model-list compatible है। हर model को tools, streaming, vision और computer-use compatibility test से गुजरना होगा।

## Source code मिला

Kilo का relevant MIT source:

- [CustomProviderDialog.tsx](https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode/blob/main/packages/kilo-vscode/webview-ui/src/components/settings/CustomProviderDialog.tsx) — form, auto-fetch, model search, multi-select, edit flow।
- [ProvidersTab.tsx](https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode/blob/main/packages/kilo-vscode/webview-ui/src/components/settings/ProvidersTab.tsx) — provider list, edit और remove।
- [Kilo custom-provider documentation](https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode/blob/main/packages/kilo-docs/pages/code-with-ai/agents/custom-models.md) — provider ID, base URL, API key, model discovery और manual models।
- [OpenClaw custom-provider docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-tools/custom-providers) — native `models.providers` configuration, adapter types और secret references।

हम Kilo का React UI wholesale merge नहीं करेंगे। उसकी interaction logic native Zero Control UI में port होगी, ताकि Zero में एक ही UI और एक ही Gateway रहे।

## Developers को नया काम

- **Qoder / CodeEd — PM-00:** Gateway-side provider manager contract, protected secret handling, server-side model discovery, test/activate/rollback, और safe `zero-gw` migration।
- **OpenCode — PM-01:** Qoder के contract के बाद native Provider Manager page।
- **Gemini:** provider feature नहीं है; वह केवल developer है और team acceptance matrix पर काम जारी रखेगा।

Qoder को यह भेजें:

```text
Qoder, start PM-00. Read E:\zero\plans\zero-provider-manager-roadmap.md, TASK_BOARD.md, PROJECT_MEMORY.md and the AGENT_CHAT.txt tail. Build the Gateway-side Provider Manager contract: list/create/update/test/discover/activate/disable/delete custom providers using native OpenClaw config patch and protected secret references. Keep zero-gw live until a replacement provider passes test and activation. Model discovery must occur server-side; never return or log an API key. Document the RPC contract in reports/backend/provider-manager-contract.md and update the board/log.
```

# PM-01 Implementation & Verification Summary

As **OpenCode**, the Kilo Code VS Code extension provider management workflow has been integrated into Zero’s native OpenClaw-themed Models experience in [`app/zero-control-ui/`](file:///e:/zero/app/zero-control-ui/) conforming to the PM-00 contract and Gateway-native RPC architecture.

---

### Key Capabilities Delivered

1. **Native Surface & Navigation Integration:**
   - Registered the native **Models & Providers** page (`/plugin?plugin=zero-control-ui&id=models`, order `32`, icon `cpu`) alongside Zero Team (`/plugin?plugin=zero-control-ui&id=team`, order `30`, icon `users`).
   - Links seamlessly between the Team surface and the Models surface without modifying core read-only upstream routes (`upstream/` remains untouched).

2. **Full Provider Lifecycle Management:**
   - **List:** Displays cards for configured providers with host endpoint, API adapter, credential status badges, model tags, and referencing roles.
   - **Add / Edit:** Modal with input validation (`^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,63}$`, HTTPS enforcement except for local loopback), adapter selection (`openai-completions`, `openai-responses`, `anthropic-messages`, `ollama`), and write-only API key handling.
   - **Disable / Delete:** Unbinds model references from defaults; protected guard prevents deletion or disablement of the bootstrap provider (`zero-gw`).

3. **Zero Secret Leakage (Write-Only API Keys):**
   - API keys are submitted directly to Gateway protected storage via `secrets.store.set(..., kind: "secret")` with host scoping.
   - Stored in configuration exclusively as a `SecretRef` (`{ source: "store", provider: "default", id: "ZERO_PROVIDER_<ID>_API_KEY" }`).
   - Raw keys are wiped immediately from memory and never returned in browser state, config reads, or outbound browser fetch calls.

4. **Server-Side Model Discovery Drawer (Kilo Code Workflow):**
   - Dispatches `zero.providers.discover` RPC via the Gateway (no browser-to-provider network calls).
   - Real-time search filtering by model name/ID.
   - Bulk actions: **Select All Filtered** and **Deselect All**.
   - Automatic model capability tags: Reasoning (`/reasoning|thinking|flash|r1|nemotron/i`) and Vision (`/vl|vision|image|4o|super-vl/i`).
   - Manual model entry row to add custom unlisted models.
   - Applies model updates atomically via `config.patch`.

5. **Upstream Connectivity Testing & Safe Activation:**
   - Dispatches `zero.providers.test` returning round-trip latency and discovered model counts.
   - Activation pre-flights connectivity, snapshots current defaults, retains previous primary in fallbacks, and automatically rolls back if health verification fails.

6. **Model Defaults Preservation:**
   - Top card manages routing defaults:
     - **Primary Model:** Auto (Host Default) + discovered provider models.
     - **Utility Model:** Auto Utility Model + discovered provider models.
     - **Fallback Model:** Configurable fallback model.
     - **Thinking Default:** `Default`, `Off`, `Low`, `Medium`, `High`, `Max`.
     - **Fast Mode Default:** `Default (Auto)`, `Fast Mode`, `Standard Mode`.

7. **Bootstrap Protection (`zero-gw`):**
   - `zero-gw` is explicitly flagged `isProtected: true`. Deleting or disabling `zero-gw` is disabled with explanatory tooltips, ensuring the gateway maintains its functional baseline (`z-ai/glm-5.3-flash` and `nvidia/nemotron-3.5-super-vl-120b-a12b`).

8. **UI-04 Reconnect Correction:**
   - Stale computer snapshots are cleared on disconnect or generation change, resetting status to `"checking"`.

---

# OpenCode — PM-01 Senior Review Follow-up & UI-04 Completion

All senior review follow-up items (F1–F7) for [PM-01](file:///e:/zero/reports/ui/provider-manager-ui.md) and the assistant-first workflow requirements for [UI-04](file:///e:/zero/reports/ui/assistant-workflow.md) are complete, verified, and published to the live Gateway runtime.

---

### Key Deliverables & Fixes

1. **Multi-Agent Activation Engine (§3.4 & §3.4a — F1)**:
   - [`activateProvider`](file:///e:/zero/app/zero-control-ui/browser.js) snapshots existing model bindings across `agents.defaults` and `agents.entries` (`zero`, `researcher`, `operator`).
   - Dispatches a single atomic `config.patch` updating defaults and each agent entry with fallback retention, ensuring explicit specialist overrides (e.g., Operator) cleanly follow the route.

2. **Post-Activation Probe & Automatic Rollback (§3.4 & §8 — F2)**:
   - Activation dispatches `models.probe` for agent `zero` (timeout: 30s).
   - On probe failure, timeout, or error, an inverse `config.patch` is immediately dispatched against the new `baseHash` returned by the activation response, including exact `replacePaths` for modified nested fallback arrays.

3. **Multi-Role Disablement (§3.5 — F3/F4)**:
   - Disabling unbinds the provider from defaults and all referencing agent entries (`agents.entries.{zero,researcher,operator}`), substituting a verified replacement model (from bootstrap `zero-gw`) while preserving the provider definition and SecretRef.
   - `referencedBy` enumerates entry fallbacks and entry `utilityModel`, preventing premature deletion or silent unbinding failures.

4. **Unsupported "Auto" Sentinel Removal (F3)**:
   - Completely eradicated literal string `"auto"` from routing payloads. Model selections map strictly to valid model references (`${providerId}/${modelId}`), satisfying OpenClaw schema requirements.

5. **Legacy Provider Settings Surface Replacement (F5)**:
   - Audited static compile-time routes in `upstream/openclaw/ui/src/app-routes.ts` (`/settings/model-providers` and `/settings/model-setup`).
   - Implemented `installLegacyRouteInterceptor` client-side interceptor that captures click and `popstate` events targeting legacy provider routes and transparently reroutes users to `/plugin?plugin=zero-control-ui&id=models` without modifying read-only `upstream/`.

6. **UI-04 Reconnect State Alignment (H-06) & Assistant Workflow**:
   - Monitored `context.host.connection.generation` and `connected` transitions in [`mountTeamPage`](file:///e:/zero/app/zero-control-ui/browser.js).
   - On disconnect, clears cached hold snapshots and renders a checking status. On reconnection or generation increment, forces a fresh invalidation and reload of computer control, skills, and workspaces.
   - Provides canonical Lead session (`zero`) conversation resumption or creation (`sessions.create`), specialist activity chipping, and connected built-in links (`/chat`, `/skills`, `/settings/memory`, `/activity`, `/workboard`, `/sessions`, `/usage`, `/settings/agents`, `/worktrees`, `/systems`, `/plugin?plugin=zero-control-ui&id=models`).
   - Retained the intake-reviewed Workshop skill candidate `subagent-echo-check` (revision `08290405…`).

---
